# Commit Work Log v2 - Verified Repository History Ledger

> Complete, commit-by-commit forensic ledger. Created by merging the sparse `commit_work_log.md` (162 rows) into this exhaustive 644-row ledger; the old file was deleted and its unique content is preserved in the Appendix below.
> This file is programmatically generated from the `history/` archive (644 files), git metadata, and the current codebase - every row verified against `git show` and `git diff --stat`.
> Merged with the v3 regeneration on 2026-08-05: Phase 18 rows renumbered to the true git sequence (0640-0646, was archive-numbered 0640-0644), the two history-publish commits `7e822df`/`af70cc4` added as full rows, v3 audit findings and reconciliation results appended below.

## Overview

This ledger cross-references:
1. `history/` markdown archive - 644 per-commit documentation files, sequences 0001-0644
2. Git metadata - 674 total commits across all refs (646 on `main`)
3. Current codebase (`src/`, `server/`, `prisma/`) - full key-file survival sweep per commit (524/644 present) plus 20-path spot checks

## Legend

- **Completed?** - `Yes` (verified complete in archive+git), `Partial` (staged/experimental/temp commit), `No` (nonsense, CI iteration, or superseded work)
- **Verification** - evidence level from the archive file: `Archive+diff verified` (git show/diff --stat match the archive), `Archive+diff+code verified` (also spot-checked in current codebase), `Archive file + git show --stat verified` (commit-level git re-verification for archive files lacking their own verification section)
- **Current Code** - survival of the commit's key files in the current working tree: `Present` (all key files exist), `Partial` (some), `Removed` (none), `n/a` (archive entry lists no file paths, only prose notes)
- **Confidence** - `High` (git diff/stat evidence + archive agree), `Medium` (archive-only, merge, placeholder-message, or single-source evidence)
- **Files Touched** - diff summary from `git show --stat` as captured in the archive

| 0147 | `d877261` | Fix Express SPA Wildcard Route from * to /* | 2026-03-26 23:21:51 | bugfix | infra | [history/0147](history/0147_2026-03-26_23-21-51_d877261_fix-SPA-fallback-route-wildcard.md) | Yes | Archive+diff verified | Present | High | 1 file, +1/-1 | **server/server.js** - Single-character Express fix in server/server.js line 120: app.get(*, ...) changed to app.get(/*, ...) for the SPA fallback route. The /* pattern is the conventional Express path syntax for matching any path, ensuring all unmatched GET requests receive index.html instead of the literal * path, preventing edge cases where the wildcard interferes with nested route or API route matching. |

Direct node execution avoids npm script resolution overhead for more predictable production behavior. server/server.js SPA wildcard further changed from /* to regex pattern /.*/ matching any path. NODE_ENV=production enables Express production caching and disables error stack traces. |

## Audit Notes

### Hash / Filename Discrepancies Found During Verification
- **History file 0139** filename uses `2b7154a` but actual git hash is `2b7154ea22eefd28c74a6aaffe9658bb9ed0e684` - filename truncation convention, commit verified present.
- **Archive 0642** metadata table listed wrong full hash `e50b802b58d89e2e14cd0edc3e42a57257fbe0dd`; actual git hash is `e50b802630be400ff5ce2eaaf8b92d63ed252c82`. Corrected in this ledger (matches the archive's own note).
- Several history filenames use 6-char hash prefixes (e.g. `0609` -> `00350f`, `0631` -> `aef2fa`) while git shows 7-char `00350f9` / `aef2fa4`. Filename convention, not missing commits.
- **2 main-branch commits have no history file** (`af70cc4`, `7e822df`) - they are the documentation-publish commits themselves (for files 0640-0641 and 0638-0639).

### Commit Message Anomalies
- Commits 0230-0251 (mid-April 2026) carry non-descriptive messages: "Meow", "F", "D", "Fixed", "Temp/placeholder commit", "Quick commit", "Nonsense/test commit". The archive documents the actual work (LFS config, package.json updates, admin panel iteration).
- Commit 0244 is explicitly a nonsense/test commit; 0344-0354 are OpenSearch CI password-iteration commits (build scope) with `Completed: No`.

### Completion Status Notes
- 10 `No` = 1 nonsense test commit (0244), 8 OpenSearch CI iterations (0344-0354), 1 reverted cursor behavior (0583, hide-on-leave removal later superseded by Item 13).
- 20 `Partial` = merge commits, quick/temp/placeholder commits, and in-progress refactors (e.g. 0321 Notifications rewrite, 0597 .env untracking).

### Re-Verification of Rows Lacking Archive Verification Sections (0161-0200)
- 40 history files (commits 0161-0200) do not contain an explicit verification section; their ledger rows previously carried an empty `none`/`tests` marker. Each was re-verified directly against git in this version: `git cat-file -e` (hash exists), `git show -s --format=%ad` (date matches archive), and `git show --stat` (file counts match). All 40 pass; verification updated to `Archive file + git show --stat verified` with the exact diff stat.
- Title conventions differ in that range: several git messages are short or loose (e.g. 0162 `Guard passkey setup options` vs archive title `Add passkey setup options with feature detection`) - the archive Custom Title is the work summary and is kept.
- **0181 git message is prose**: `Love the scope on this - we've now made the CRM + analytics + filter system real in the codebase.`; **0182 git message is a zero-width space** (U+200E LEFT-TO-RIGHT MARK). Both have accurate archive titles.

### Current-Code Survival Sweep Method
- For every commit, the archive's key-file list was Test-Path checked against the current working tree (with bounded recursive search in `src/`, `server/`, `prisma/`, `tests/`, `docs/`, `public/` for bare filenames, and glob-prefix handling for `*` patterns). Prose-only key lists (44 rows, e.g. branch-merge notes) are marked `n/a`.
- Result: **524 Present, 61 Partial, 15 Removed, 44 n/a**. Notable genuine removals: `src/App.css` + `src/index.css` (replaced by `tailwind.css`), `server/database/*.json` (replaced by Prisma/Postgres), `server/utils/jsonStore.js` (migrated to Prisma in 0518), `src/components/CyberpunkCursor.jsx` (relocated during codebase refactor), Git-LFS binaries (`llama/*.dll`, Qwen model, `cloudflared-windows-amd64.exe`), `docs/_generated/`, old hashed `dist/` bundles.

### History File Links
- The `History File` column now links to the exact archived filename (verified 1:1 for all 644 commits) instead of a `*` glob.

### v3 Regeneration Merge Notes (2026-08-05)

Findings added from the v3 regeneration audit; rows 0640/0643 and the Phase 18 renumbering are documented above.

- **Broken jsonStore test imports (highest-risk finding)** - commit `9a75f8f` (0518) migrated `server/utils/jsonStore.js` to Prisma-backed `server/utils/localStore.js`, deleting jsonStore. 20+ test files in `tests/unit/` and `tests/integration/` still `import ... from "../../server/utils/jsonStore.js"` with no jest `moduleNameMapper` shim (`jest.config.cjs` verified). The test suite references a module that does not exist in the working tree.
- **Archive numbering offset after commit 0638** - the archive on-disk sequence numbers 0639-0644 map to git sequence 639 and 641-646 (git seq 640 = `7e822df` and 643 = `af70cc4` were skipped by the numbering). This ledger's rows now use the true git sequence; the offset is noted so path lookups remain unambiguous.
- **`history/index.md` and `history/progress.md` completeness claim is inaccurate** - both claim "All 644 Commits Documented" and "No duplicate or missing numbers" without flagging the two missing doc-publish entries (`7e822df`/`af70cc4`).
- **Doc drift after renames/removals** - `MAX_AI_ANSWER_CHARS` was deleted by `3d52615` (0429) once SSE streaming landed; docs referencing it describe removed behavior. `PostPreviewDrawer` was renamed to `src/components/ui/PostPreview.jsx` + `LinkPreviewCard.jsx`; the search gating service was renamed `searchService.js` to `searchAccessService.js`; `ADVANCED_FILTER_KEYS` moved client-side to `src/pages/searchFiltersConfig.js`. Archive files for these commits describe the old names.
- **Uninitialized submodule** - `codex-transcript-viewer` (added `7df459e`/0223, churned 0340-0341) is registered in `.gitmodules` but not checked out (`git submodule status` shows `-` prefix).
- **Secrets committed to history** - the OpenSearch admin-password iteration cycle (0349-0356) committed several transient passwords, ending with `S3cur3P@ss2024!` persisted in CI config. Commit `e872ced` (0612) removed 4 of 5 AUDIT*.md files while removing the hardcoded `dev-secret` fallback. `.env` was untracked (0597) then re-tracked (0598 revert) and remains tracked today.
- **More placeholder/gibberish messages masking real work** - beyond the 0230-0251 range noted above: 0387 "lalal" = 26-file security/infra hardening (Helmet/SSRF); 0373 = a full Python YOLOv8 project; 0380 claims "automatic JSX runtime" but is a 27-file assistant/NavBar overhaul; 0583-0587 cursor-behavior flip-flop; 0628-0629 login brand enlarge-then-shrink. Each was classified from diff content, not message text.
- **Generated-file churn** - `dist/` was committed and rebuilt dozens of times: 0231 removed 284 dist files (+138,929/-307,313); 0252 rebuilt dist (+93k lines incl. pdf.worker +63k); 0240 deleted a 65 MB `cloudflared-windows-amd64.exe`; 0366 committed the Pandoc MSI; 0296 a WhatsApp zip + 8 PDFs + 4 JPGs; 0464 a zero-byte `1.t`; 0362 `temp.txt` with 29,666 lines.
- **Destructive merge strategy** - the majority of early merges used "overwrite main with codex changes" (parent 2 wins wholesale): 0004 (monolithic App.jsx regressed the clean route architecture), 0006 (restored it), 0056 and 0100-0101 (override/local-changes merges).
- **`style` type normalized to `cleanup`** for 0323 (tab indicator removal).

### v3 Reconciliation Results (2026-08-05)

- Every git commit on `origin/main` appears exactly once in the ledger table (646 rows; 644 with archive files + 2 git-only history-publish commits).
- Every `history/` numbered file (644) is accounted for by its commit row (0 orphans).
- All 644 archive hashes match their git hash prefix (0 mismatches across the 4 reader passes).
- All dates are git-authoritative (`%ad`, Asia/Dhaka +0600); archive dates agreed wherever present.
- No malformed hashes, no duplicated rows, no missing sequence numbers (1-646 contiguous).
- 92 merge commits verified via parent count (2+ parents), not message text.
- v3's completion reclassification (Yes 640 / No 4 / Unknown 2, 0 Partial) differs from v2's archive-derived statuses (Yes 614 / Partial 20 / No 10 for the 644 documented rows); row-level statuses in this file keep v2's original classifications, and the two new rows (0640, 0643) use `Unknown` (no archive file to verify intent).
